- `film` · the lens' blur on a strongly defocused foreground (shared code, `src/core/blur.ts`), from the pointer of round 3 of the places (rounds 20 to 25; `eye`, round 24, `R3-1` issue 8: "a painted smear with posterised edges"). Tested on 2026-11-26 at 11:10 UTC on six final-tier 4K stills at `c7bf3298`, each frame as it is and with the lens' blur off. MEASURED: the rise's water at frame 9563 is the same to the byte; at frame 1546 (`ribs-3`) the rock in focus is the same (fine detail ratio 1.00 to 1.06) and only the far blade is 2 to 3 times sharper with the blur off; at frame 7579 (`spine-1`) the subject is near the same (1.1 to 1.6), the arch and sky behind 3 to 6 times, the near ground about 2 times, and the ground's streaks remain either way. With the earlier finding (the shutter is closed in every unit but the Heart at `film.maxSmearShare` 1e-5) the second half of the pointer is closed: rock and water that smear in sharp parts of the frame are each place's own texture and stay on that place's item (the Ribs' rock, the Spine's surface, the Reveal's water). The first half is confirmed as the lens' own fault: on the far defocused pebbles of `eye-1` (frame 9003, the crop x 1200 to 2400, y 1560 to 2160 of `out/stills/eye/still-key-eye-1-uhd-final.jpg`) the blur leaves hard outlines between the stones and a faint lattice of about 4 px. Being fixed in this phase by `lens_builder` (branch `agent/lens`, brief `out/work/briefs/p5/lens.md`, 2 h from 11:42 UTC): the cause first, then the fix, with what is in focus and every closed-lens frame unchanged to the byte and at most 1.0 s more a frame at 4K. After the merge every finished unit's keys are rendered anew and looked at beside the kept ones, as "Freshness" says, and the measures are run again. If the fix is not in by the second whole-film round it is carried to the first polish round. Update of 2026-11-26 13:21 UTC: fixed in shared code by the lens' builder and merged as `49918399` (the decision of 13:21 UTC): the layers are laid in the last fine pass, the 4 px lattice falls from 12.5 to 1.2, what is in focus is unchanged within 1 to 3 levels on at most 0.13 percent of its pixels. What is left of this item: everything 2 to 6 px out of focus is now softer than it was, so each finished place's keys are rendered anew and looked at beside the kept ones (ordered 13:25 UTC), with one fix a place, the shot's aperture, where a key lost too much. Update of 2026-11-26 14:58 UTC, the freshness check closed: after `49918399` fifteen keys of six places stood lower against their kept stills (0.639 to 0.894 over the whole frame) and M3 was red on `ribs-3` (0.7459) and `spine-1` (0.748). - Open, `ribs-3`, on `main` since 2026-11-26 02:09 UTC (6b36521e): the kite-wings that pass close to the lens at the colony are moved 40 to 60 m off by the sea's owner (`ribs_sea_owner_2`, version 1 of wave 2), after the fliers and the colony drew severe issues in round 10 (R1 pack B, R3 pack B). The detail is carried by the colony on its ledges. The settled sentence is in `settled["ribs-3"]` of `config/showcase.json`, word for word.
- Open, `spine-4`, on `main` since 2026-11-26 02:21 UTC (d7c67ab1): the herd in the hollow is restaged by `spine_storm_owner_2` (version 1 of wave 2), after the ridge-runners drew severe issues in round 9. The six animals sit further in, five grouped at the left wall and one standing against the mouth, lit only from the mouth behind them as near silhouettes. The detail is carried by the place: the rimed mouth, the ice floor, the storm outside. The settled sentence is in `settled["spine-4"]` of `config/showcase.json`, word for word.
- Open, `eye-1`, `eye-2`, `eye-4`, on `main` since 2026-11-26 02:14 UTC (ecb3bc47): the drinkers of the Eye are restaged by `eye_owner_2` (version 1 of wave 2), ordered 01:10 UTC after they drew severe issues in round 12. `eye-1`: four animals side on in the shallows, 21 to 31 m off, the camera 6.5 m back on the gravel with the foreground soft. `eye-2`: three small animals 31 to 52 m out beyond the mats, in silhouette. `eye-4`: two from behind, cropped by the foot of the frame and out of focus. The detail is carried by the place: the shallows, the mats, the dark water. The settled sentence is in `settled` of `config/showcase.json` for each of the three keys, word for word.

## For Brandon to decide

- The Spine's thunder (note 8: "The same sound serves every strike."). It plays on strikes 1 and 4 of the six, because the bed, the life sound and two strikes are the 4 cues `audio.maxCuesPerPlace` allows a place and the Builder may not raise a limit. For all six: say so, and the limit for the Spine becomes 8, or a sound played several times counts once. Nothing new is made either way and no credits are spent.
- X. The 4K 60 fps master may not upload as it is: X's written web limits are 1920 x 1200, 40 fps and 25 Mbps, and 4K upload for subscribers rests on one 2025 post by X Engineering. The 1080p copy is the safe file to post. Nothing in the build changes for this. See "Platform".

Decided by Brandon on 2026-11-25 (note 14) and off this list: the eight sounds kept with a fault (they stay, no third take), how loud the sounds sit (the levels of the Heart clip stay and the other 17 follow them), the second spot of the music (the cleaned track stays, the lighter cut stays set aside).
Decided by Brandon on 2026-11-26 (note 17) and off this list: the script. It is final as last shown in the thread, with "It was a titan." in the Reveal, and all eight clips are made from it.
- The shutter (2026-11-26 04:34 UTC). On a fast camera move the camera's shutter now closes, so every frame of the film is as sharp as a still and nothing moves as four copies; water, cloud, animals and sparks keep their blur. The Heart is left as you approved it. To get the full 180 degree camera blur back everywhere set `film.maxSmearShare` to 0 in `config/showcase.json`; drawn cleanly it needs many more samples on fast frames, some 30 to 40 machine-hours more for the 4K film, and the Reveal's first key then falls under its fine-detail floor. To give the Heart the same rule as the rest, empty `film.fullShutterUnits`.

Wave 3, the owners' first lists (04:58 to 05:05 UTC; each looked at its own frames beside the approved pictures before changing anything), worst first. Ribs ground: the blades are smooth clay blobs with a moire hatch, the opening has no fog bank and no change in its first two seconds, ribs-0's shingle is ringed loaves, ribs-3 is a pyramid of courses, ribs-4 black stacks, the weed wall grey; land cost 10.9 s. Ribs sea: ribs-2's feeders are dark brown cut-outs, the colony one flat cream drawing, the bore a grey fog bank with no dark face, the opening sea a teal sheet, the drowned one small and dark, the water too cold. Breath ground: a two-tone shelf with saw-tooth edges, the vapour a thin see-through column (new technique: a dense body with a lit top), breath-0's channel a flat lake, breath-4 without plume or lip rock; cost 23.7 s. Breath life: the plume a dry orange ball, the water too dark by number, breath-2's plumes far back, the wader's neck a rod, the dead one's neck arches. Marsh ground: the brine flat pink paint with ring lines (a new surface model), the sky blank, marsh-4's ground wrong, the flats a pink speckle; cost 11.8 s. Marsh life: the stalks read as palms, the dead are dark grey heaps, the stuck one smooth as a seal, the wallower on stilts, drawn rings, nothing drips. Spine ground: spine-3's sky one flat violet wash 40 levels too bright, all keys in a lavender middle, the hero spire a blade on a platform, spine-4's walls dark blue; cost 15.2 s. Spine storm: spine-3 an even violet wash with no bloom (cause: `cloudLit`), the kites opaque lilac sheets, the herd small and evenly lit, the dead one a black hull, the bolt a broad white stroke. Eye: eye-0's mats as tiger stripes, eye-2 contour swirls, grainy mist and a hard band, eye-3 no pale wall under water, eye-4 striped backs, eye-1 pebbles as beans; cost 11.6 s. Reveal: the glint grows into a tan disc by 11743, the sea cloud is hard white dashes, the cyclone combed flakes, the hold reads as a still, the land greyer and redder than approved, the bones dimmer, the vapour short, frames 9556 to 9586 flat water. Pushes work for seven owners; the Spine's ground owner, the Marsh's ground owner and the Reveal's owner were refused and hand in bundles by mail. The pack renderers `pack_renderer_r8`, `r9`, `r11`, `r12` are idle and kept for round 3.

Wave 3 of Phase 4: ten build agents, each on its own machine, all six units at once, each the last owner of the folders of a wave 2 owner and each starting from `main` at `9741a5fb`, which holds version 3 of all ten second owners. Their briefs are `out/work/briefs/p4c/PLACE-OWNERS-3.md` (one sheet for all, written by `out/work/briefs/p4c/sheet3.py` from wave 2's sheet, with what is different for a last owner, where the second owners fell short, the measures, the shutter rule and the costs) and `out/work/briefs/p4c/<agent>.md` (written by `out/work/briefs/p4c/make.py`; each leads with the second owner's own list of what is still wrong, worst first, and then the Builder's read of version 3 beside the approved pictures, from `out/work/briefs/p4c/notes/`). Sixteen agents run: the ten owners and six render agents, which is `sandbox.maxAgents`; the packs, measures, critics and jurors of round 2 pass through the six slots that are not the owners'. Round 2 (rounds 14 to 19) judges the commit they start from, and its words go to each owner as a message when its round is in. Each owner sends its own list and its requests for pictures within 25 minutes, pushes its branch and its looks every 30 minutes (or sends a bundle), hands back three versions by minutes 80, 160 and 225 (about 06:11, 07:31 and 08:36 UTC) and stops by 08:51 UTC. Its last version is final: after it nobody tunes the place again except to fix a check.

Its own verdict, kept for the next owner: the melt at the line is a net of cracks round dark plates and not ropes, the horizon at heart-1 is one orange strip, the seam at heart-3 is a flat strip, the pools and the rampart are not started.

Ended and merged into `main` in Phase 3, the first owners of the Heart (each 3 h, four versions; the last hand-backs between 07:18 and 07:32 UTC): `heart_land_owner` (`agent/heart-land` at `c9a7cca`, merged as `4ec487c`), `heart_air_owner` (`13eca8e`, merged as `923cec1`), `heart_fall_owner` (`ebb6248` merged as `c61d5d8`, then `8110ffe` merged as `e4c75c7`), `heart_walkers_owner` (`ec17222`, merged as `74b8368`), `heart_sunk_owner` (`beb0d0b`, merged as `4fead3b`), `heart_light_owner` (`bfc35f5`, merged as `ec707d9`, light version 2). `heart_lake_owner` ended at 06:39 UTC; its folder was split between `heart_crust_owner` and `heart_lava_owner`. Ended and merged into `main` in Phase 3: `engine_focus_builder` (final at 06:54 UTC after 92 minutes, `agent/core-dof` at `b171d9c`, merged as `31710fb`: depth of field as a signed circle of confusion carried in the accumulation alpha and a gather blur at quarter size before bloom, a `focus` view, `--aperture` and `--focus` on `npm run still` and `npm run compare`, the seen depth as 24 bit so G10 passes, and `--hold` and `npm run sheet -- --held` for a held camera with the world moving on. MEASURED by the agent: 830 of 830 tests, about 1.0 s added to a uhd still and 0.24 s to an hd still on 2 cores, lens closed bit-identical to before. MEASURED by the Builder after the merge: types clean, G10 pass, determinism pass, 830 of 830 tests. Still wrong, by its own list: one very bright pixel far out of focus leaves a faint pale square, thin things in front of a much more blurred wall stay a little sharp, smoke blurs as the surface behind it.)

Refits asked of the place builders after the Builder's own compare of each key against its picture, 20 minutes each: `place_marsh_builder` (answered 01:27 UTC, `7168ea3`, merged as `c086bac`), `place_heart_builder` (answered 01:31 UTC after 9 minutes, `487cee2`, merged as `6438fe9`), `place_breath_builder` (answered 01:37 UTC after 16 minutes, `ec81ce7`, merged as `13552ba`). Their briefs are in `out/work/briefs/` and share one sheet with the schemas of `cut/MAP.json`, `cut/CUTLIST.json` and `audio/mix/mix.json`, which is copied into `tools/README.md`. Each reports with a `git bundle` attached.

Sandbox agents and the push: `gh auth setup-git` fails in most sandbox VMs ("not logged into any GitHub hosts"), and a plain `git push` then works in most of them and fails in some. Every brief says: push, and if it fails attach a `git bundle` to the report. Both routes have been used and both work.

Ended with a record: `render_meter_01` and `render_meter_02` (both answered by 22:15 UTC: every step ran, G3 passed on each VM, their stills and budget files are under "Frames across VMs" and "Render budget").

Ended with a record in Phase 2: the six blockout critics, `blockout_critic_r1_a` to `_c` (round 1, answered by 01:48 UTC) and `blockout_critic_r2_a` to `_c` (round 2, sent 02:44 UTC, two answered before 02:50 UTC and the last at 02:55 UTC after one reminder; none was replaced). Their answers are in `scorecards/round-001/` and `scorecards/round-002/`.

Ended and merged into `main` in Phase 2: `film_chunk_renderer_1` to `film_chunk_renderer_6`, their second job (asked 02:38 UTC, all six answered between 02:44 and 02:46 UTC: 112 chunks of the blockout film at the draft tier in the preview format at 30 frames a second, 5931 frames, from commit `c8792c5`, the build round 2 judges. Each reported 0.35 to 0.48 s a frame, one run and no error. Every chunk record carries the engine hash of that build, and the Builder joined them with the mix into `out/work/film/the-carcass-preview.mp4`, 73.7 MB, checked with ffprobe); `place_breath_builder`, a follow-up (asked 02:25 UTC, answered 02:29 UTC, commit `708dff3`, merged from a bundle: the exhale is ten separate puffs that rise and drift where it was one opaque dome and column, and its cap, apron and pool are half the size, so no frame of `breath-02` or `breath-03` has a flat pale third. Asked by the Builder before round 2 because round 1 failed the Spine for the same thing. Checked after the merge: the sheets of both shots and the compare of key `breath-4`. Left: the column reads thin at its peak and the vapour dome is still one opaque shape, smaller); `place_marsh_builder`, a follow-up (asked 02:25 UTC, answered 02:30 UTC, commit `abf9e5d`, merged from a bundle: the burst in `marsh-03` is a dome that swells and breaks into about 70 drops and puffs with two rings on the pool, where it was a solid cone and column across the middle of the frame, and the camera ends 0.4 m past the key where it ended 1.3 m past. By its own count the largest joined shape of the burst covers 6.8 percent of the frame. Checked after the merge: the sheet of the shot and the compare of key `marsh-1`); `place_eye_builder` (answered 02:27 UTC after 24 of its 25 minutes with the fixes of round 1 on the Eye, commits `8f5966b` and `76559f2`, merged from a bundle as `e6394d2`. `eye-05` is one flight from the key to straight above the centre of the lake and ends on the water and the nine rings of the contract; by its own count the largest change of speed between two frames is 1.94 percent of the peak, 292 m a second. The establishing key sits 34 m up and 115 m behind the shoreline, so the lake spans the frame. Checked by the Builder after the merge: all five keys in `npm run compare`, the sheets of `eye-01`, `eye-04` and `eye-05`, and stills of frames 9500 to 9580. MEASURED by the Builder across the cut at the draft tier: the outer ring's edge is 370.5 px from the centre on frame 9540 and 368.5 px on frame 9541, shrinking 1 to 2 px a frame on both sides; the two frames differ by 2.1 levels on average, their neighbours by 2.0. Changed by the Builder after the merge: `eye-01` travels 176 m where the builder's glide travelled 44 m and read as a still, and the track of `eye-04` settles 1.2 m past the key so the near animal covers less of the frame. Left: the establishing frame's deep water is a thinner band than the picture's and its animals about half the picture's size); `place_spine_builder` (answered 02:25 UTC after 17 of its 25 minutes with the fixes of round 1 on the Spine, commits `683920e` and `518a38e`, merged from a bundle as `3a8b2a5`. The carcass is a low body on its side with quills, a clawed foreleg and a hind leg, and the camera stops at the key so it never grows past about 30 percent of the frame; nothing crosses the lens in the canal; `spine-05` is a steady push of 13 m toward a far mouth that the canal now has, 232 m along the ridge; `spine-02` travels 60 m and `spine-04` 302 m; every shot travels the same way along the ridge. Checked by the Builder after the merge: all five keys in `npm run compare` and the sheets of `spine-02` to `spine-05`. Left, by its report: the cut from `spine-02` to `spine-03` still jumps 580 m back along the crest, because the approved picture has the carcass on the saddle with the cave ahead; `spine-04` is the quietest shot (the spire grows 1.18 times); a second spire shows at the left edge of the far mouth); `place_ribs_builder` (its follow-up answered 02:24 UTC inside its 8 minutes, commit `3f82f38`, merged from a bundle: the surf is four narrow pale streaks, 6 to 12 m wide and about 20 m apart, where it was one band, so no frame of `ribs-01` has a flat pale lower half. Checked by the Builder after the merge: frames 740, 780 and 820 and the compare of key `ribs-0`); `place_ribs_builder` (answered 02:16 UTC after 9 of its 20 minutes with the fix of round 1 on the Ribs, commit `61dc42c`, merged from a bundle as `5b3085b`. `ribs-01` and `ribs-02` are one function of time, so the cut at 1041 to 1042 falls inside a single move: by its own count the speed is 28.82 m a second into frame 1041 and 28.74 into 1042, and the largest change of speed between two frames is 0.1 percent of the peak. The approach runs 1331 m along the line of sight of the establishing picture while the lens opens from 19.8 to 44.1 degrees, so the near left rib, the channel and the arch rock are in frame from frame 0. Checked by the Builder after the merge: the compare of key `ribs-0`, which is unchanged, and the sheets of both shots. Left, by its report: far out the near left rib reads as a pale blade and not as a dark bank, the arch rock sits at 65 percent of the width at frame 0 where the picture has 85, and from about 12 to 14 s the lower half of the frame is the white surf band); `blockout_critic_r1_c` (answered 01:48 UTC: score 4, three severe, four major and two minor issues, in `scorecards/round-001/R4b-3.json`); `blockout_critic_r1_b` (answered 01:48 UTC: score 10, no issues, in `scorecards/round-001/R4b-2.json`); `blockout_critic_r1_a` (answered 01:48 UTC: score 6, three severe and one major issue, in `scorecards/round-001/R4b-1.json`); `film_chunk_renderer_6` (answered by 01:50 UTC after 6 to 8 minutes with a tar of its share of the 112 chunks of the blockout film at commit `30d8128` (MEASURED: 0.36 to 0.51 s a frame)); `film_chunk_renderer_5` (answered by 01:50 UTC after 6 to 8 minutes with a tar of its share of the 112 chunks of the blockout film at commit `30d8128` (MEASURED: 0.36 to 0.51 s a frame)); `film_chunk_renderer_4` (answered by 01:50 UTC after 6 to 8 minutes with a tar of its share of the 112 chunks of the blockout film at commit `30d8128` (MEASURED: 0.36 to 0.51 s a frame)); `film_chunk_renderer_3` (answered by 01:50 UTC after 6 to 8 minutes with a tar of its share of the 112 chunks of the blockout film at commit `30d8128` (MEASURED: 0.36 to 0.51 s a frame)); `film_chunk_renderer_2` (answered by 01:50 UTC after 6 to 8 minutes with a tar of its share of the 112 chunks of the blockout film at commit `30d8128` (MEASURED: 0.36 to 0.51 s a frame)); `film_chunk_renderer_1` (answered by 01:50 UTC after 6 to 8 minutes with a tar of its share of the 112 chunks of the blockout film at commit `30d8128` (MEASURED: 0.36 to 0.51 s a frame)); `place_spine_builder` (answered 01:27 UTC after 51 minutes, the blockout of the Spine in `src/places/spine/`: 14 parts and five shot cameras, merged from a bundle as `f416742`; about 245,000 triangles by its own estimate. The melt reads as a flat bright gradient with sharp edges that lights little around it (3 severe, 3 major). The fall is one thin channel where your picture has a broad cascade, and the wall round the mouth reads as level strata (2 severe, 3 major). A pink-red block of glow with a hard edge sits at the far right of the wide view (2 severe, 1 major). The feeding seam has lost the lava stream and the pool behind the animals (1 severe).
Parts finished with a fallback: none
Animals kept at a distance: none

Sound files: committed to the repo
What happens next is yours:
- To change the voice, the sound or anything you see, as many times as you like: send the review notes prompt from kit/10-KICKOFF.md.
- When the Heart is right: send the approval prompt from kit/10-KICKOFF.md. The other places and the rise to orbit are then built the same way, and nothing stops until the film is delivered.
